# Commands · R · part 9

1,490 entries, resume to review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **resume**: Pick up a feature that already exists in this forge workspace — one you paused, or one a teammate started months ago. Reads its spec, plan, notes, QA checklist and review escalations, reports where things actually stand and what is next, then stops and asks. With no argument, presents the workspace's features and asks which. Read-only — changes no code, checks out no branches, dispatches nothing. ([aiforging/aiforging](https://github.com/aiforging/aiforging/blob/HEAD/commands/resume.md) · ★5)
- **resume**: Continue a paused route, or restart a stopped one from its record. ([arafathusayn/herdsman](https://github.com/arafathusayn/herdsman/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/resume.md) · ★5)

- **resume**: Resume work where the last session left off — read the key project documents, decide the single most important thing to do now, announce it — then do what the owner wrote under the word (a question is answered, not followed by work; a bare "resume" starts the chosen direction). Use when the human says "continue", "let's continue", "what's next", "where did we leave off", "resume", "резюм", "резюме", "pick up where we left off", "продолжи", "продолжим", "что дальше" — and ALWAYS when "resume" (or "резюм") is the FIRST word of the human's message with the task written below it: the skill runs F… ([MikalaiKryvusha/KAGO](https://github.com/MikalaiKryvusha/KAGO/blob/HEAD/.roo/commands/resume.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **resume**: Продолжить работу по плану с того места, где остановились в прошлый раз — прочитать ключевые документы проекта, выбрать главное, объявить его — а затем сделать то, что Криник написал под словом (на вопрос отвечаем, а не начинаем работу; голое «resume» запускает выбранное направление). Используй когда Криник говорит "продолжи", "продолжим с того где остановились", "что дальше", "с чего начнём", "резюм", "резюме", "continue", "resume", "let's continue", "pick up where we left off" — и ВСЕГДА, когда "resume" (или "резюм") — ПЕРВОЕ слово сообщения Криника, а под ним написана задача: навык идёт ПЕ… ([MikalaiKryvusha/KrinikCam](https://github.com/MikalaiKryvusha/KrinikCam/blob/HEAD/.roo/commands/resume.md) · ★4)

- **retro**: &gt; **命名说明**：`retro` 即 `retrospective` 的简称（agile 团队术语,母语者常说 "run a retro on X"）。本命令的核心**不是**"过一遍 issue 标 done",而是**从积累的已修复问题里找共性根因、沉淀机制改进,从根本上避免同类问题再次发生**。 &gt; &gt; **历史**：本命令早期叫 `/issueReview`,但 "review" 在编程语境太轻量（与 code review / PR review 同词,容易理解为"看一下 + 标个状态"）,且不暗示"找根因 + 防再发生"的核心目的 — 2026-05-12 改名 `/retro`,语义更精准。 ([derricktang/pm-workflow-plugin](https://github.com/derricktang/pm-workflow-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/pm/commands/retro.md) · ★2)
- **retro**: Produce sprint retrospective and lessons-learned document ([Entelligentsia/forge](https://github.com/Entelligentsia/forge/blob/HEAD/node_modules/%40entelligentsia/forgecli/dist/forge-payload/.base-pack/commands/retro.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)

- **retro**: &gt; **Primary Agents:** `@RetroFacilitator` (leads) · `@ErrorDetective` (mistake analysis) · `@KnowledgeSynthesizer` (rule propagation) &gt; **Purpose:** Close the feedback loop — turn sprint experience into permanent agent intelligence &gt; **Scope:** Sprint-end reflection, error pattern distillation, skill + agent Hard Rules updates, institutional memory ([iDorgham/Workspace-Factory](https://github.com/iDorgham/Workspace-Factory/blob/HEAD/factory/library/archive/legacy_pillars/10_engineering_devops/10_operations_qa/execution/commands/retro.md) · ★2)

- **retry**: Send a stopped task (blocked, failed, no response) out again (--by NAME, --delegated WHY, or left out: signed by the session — a retry is not a gate); the next call receives the earlier attempts. --requires: the sandbox lacked CAP — the task says so, and goes to the lock's &lt;role&gt;@&lt;cap&gt;. A session task is measured again and what its measure leaves out is named, with the adopt that settles it. --checked / --checked-red: evidence chongdae runs itself (a pass; a tests task's expected red). A --checked-red run that never reached its tests is refused. ([guinjaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaakeop/chongdae](https://github.com/guinjaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaakeop/chongdae/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/retry.md) · ★1)
